{$CLEO .c}
{$USE CLEO+}
{$USE memory}
{$USE ini}

0000: NOP

// =========================================================================
// YORIGAMI MASTER HEAP DICTIONARY (PRUNED FOR WANTED BANTER)
// =========================================================================
const
    MEM_PRIORITY_LOCK = 20
    MEM_SHION_HANDLE = 116
    MEM_SHION_PRES_HP = 160
    MEM_LAST_WANTED_BANT = 200
    MEM_RESTRICTED_AIR = 216
end

:Connect_To_Brain
wait 0
0AAA: 31@ = get_script_struct_named 'Y_Brain'
if
    31@ == 0 
then
    goto @Connect_To_Brain
end
000A: 31@ += 0x3C 
0A8D: 31@ = read_memory 31@ size 4 virtual_protect 0 
// 31@ NOW HOLDS THE MASTER POINTER.

script_name "Sh_Star"

// 0@ = Shion Actor Handle passed from ShionChat.c
0085: 10@ = 0@

// =========================================================================
// TIER 3 SUBTITLE PRIORITY INJECTION
// =========================================================================
0085: 30@ = 31@
000A: 30@ += MEM_PRIORITY_LOCK
0A8D: 16@ = read_memory 30@ size 4 virtual_protect 0
if
    16@ >= 3 // Yield to Tier 4 Sister Talk or Diner
then
    0A93: terminate_this_custom_script
end

// Preempt Tier 1 (Smoking) & Tier 2 (Ambient World Chat)
0ABA: terminate_all_custom_scripts_with_this_name "Sh_Zone"
0ABA: terminate_all_custom_scripts_with_this_name "Sh_Mny"
0ABA: terminate_all_custom_scripts_with_this_name "Sh_Idle"
0ABA: terminate_all_custom_scripts_with_this_name "Sh_Snip"
0ABA: terminate_all_custom_scripts_with_this_name "Sh_Int"
0ABA: terminate_all_custom_scripts_with_this_name "Sh_Veh"
0ABA: terminate_all_custom_scripts_with_this_name "Sh_Rad"
0ABA: terminate_all_custom_scripts_with_this_name "Sh_Sub"
0ABA: terminate_all_custom_scripts_with_this_name "Sh_Wthr"
0ABA: terminate_all_custom_scripts_with_this_name "Sh_SndC"
03EB: clear_small_messages_only

0085: 30@ = 31@
000A: 30@ += MEM_PRIORITY_LOCK
0A8C: write_memory 30@ size 4 value 3 virtual_protect 0 // Claim Tier 3 Action Priority!

// Validate player state
if or
    8256: not player $PLAYER_CHAR defined
    0E25: is_on_cutscene
    0EB7: is_on_scripted_cutscene
then
    0002: jump @Finalize_Wanted
end

01F5: $PLAYER_ACTOR = get_player_actor $PLAYER_CHAR

if or
    0118: actor $PLAYER_ACTOR dead
    0741: actor $PLAYER_ACTOR busted
then
    0002: jump @Finalize_Wanted
end

01C0: 22@ = player $PLAYER_CHAR wanted_level

// Exit if player has no wanted level
if
    8019: not 22@ > 0
then
    0002: jump @Finalize_Wanted
end

// Check Form Mode: 0 = Physical Ped, 1 = Hitodama Spirit
0085: 30@ = 31@
000A: 30@ += MEM_SHION_HANDLE
0A8D: 10@ = read_memory 30@ size 4 virtual_protect 0
if
    10@ == -1
then
    11@ = 1 // Hitodama Form
else
    11@ = 0 // Physical Ped Form
end

// Check if player is on/in a train (Models 537, 538 or driving train)
0470: 20@ = actor $PLAYER_ACTOR current_weapon
if
    0449: actor $PLAYER_ACTOR in_a_car
then
    03C0: 3@ = actor $PLAYER_ACTOR car
    0441: 4@ = car 3@ model
    if or
        0039: 4@ == 537 // Freight
        0039: 4@ == 538 // Streak
        0039: 4@ == 569 // Freight Train Flatbed
        0039: 4@ == 570 // Streak Train Trailer
        0039: 4@ == 590 // Box Freight
        09AE: actor $PLAYER_ACTOR driving_train
    then
        018C: play_sound 1058 at 0.0 0.0 0.0
        03EB: clear_small_messages_only
        0ADF: add_text_label "Y_WTRN0" text "~b~Shion: ~s~\"We're on the train, Jo'on... the police cruisers are just keeping pace along the access roads.\""
        09C1: add_next_text_to_brief_history 1
        00BC: show_text_highpriority GXT 'Y_WTRN0' time 3700 flag 1
        0001: wait 3700 ms
        0050: gosub @Check_Alive_Or_Exit

        018C: play_sound 1058 at 0.0 0.0 0.0
        03EB: clear_small_messages_only
        0ADF: add_text_label "Y_WTRN1" text "~y~Jo'on: ~s~\"Let 'em pace! A hundred tons of steel beats a cruiser every day of the week! AHAHAHA!\""
        09C1: add_next_text_to_brief_history 1
        00BC: show_text_highpriority GXT 'Y_WTRN1' time 3700 flag 1
        0001: wait 3700 ms
        0002: jump @Finalize_Wanted
    end
end

// Check if player is indoors
077E: get_active_interior_to 5@
if
    0019: 5@ > 0
then
    0209: 18@ = random_int_in_ranges 0 2
    if
        18@ == 0
    then
        018C: play_sound 1058 at 0.0 0.0 0.0
        03EB: clear_small_messages_only
        if
            0039: 11@ == 1
        then
            0ADF: add_text_label "Y_WIN0H" text "~b~Shion: ~s~\"We're hiding inside... the police outside don't know which door we took, Jo'on.\""
            09C1: add_next_text_to_brief_history 1
            00BC: show_text_highpriority GXT 'Y_WIN0H' time 3600 flag 1
        else
            0ADF: add_text_label "Y_WINT0" text "~b~Shion: ~s~\"We're indoors, Jo'on... stay away from the front windows so they don't spot us.\""
            09C1: add_next_text_to_brief_history 1
            00BC: show_text_highpriority GXT 'Y_WINT0' time 3600 flag 1
        end
        0001: wait 3600 ms
        0050: gosub @Check_Alive_Or_Exit

        018C: play_sound 1058 at 0.0 0.0 0.0
        03EB: clear_small_messages_only
        0ADF: add_text_label "Y_WINT1" text "~y~Jo'on: ~s~\"Keep your head down! If a squad car pulls up outside, we bolt out the back!\""
        09C1: add_next_text_to_brief_history 1
        00BC: show_text_highpriority GXT 'Y_WINT1' time 3500 flag 1
        0001: wait 3500 ms
    else
        018C: play_sound 1058 at 0.0 0.0 0.0
        03EB: clear_small_messages_only
        0ADF: add_text_label "Y_WND2A" text "~b~Shion: ~s~\"Watch the doors, Jo'on... if we step outside right now, there's gonna be a cop waiting.\""
        09C1: add_next_text_to_brief_history 1
        00BC: show_text_highpriority GXT 'Y_WND2A' time 3600 flag 1
        0001: wait 3600 ms
        0050: gosub @Check_Alive_Or_Exit

        018C: play_sound 1058 at 0.0 0.0 0.0
        03EB: clear_small_messages_only
        0ADF: add_text_label "Y_WND2B" text "~y~Jo'on: ~s~\"Amateurs campin' the exit! I'll shove right past 'im before he even draws his baton!\""
        09C1: add_next_text_to_brief_history 1
        00BC: show_text_highpriority GXT 'Y_WND2B' time 3500 flag 1
        0001: wait 3500 ms
    end
    0002: jump @Finalize_Wanted
end

// =========================================================================
// CONTEXT SENSORS: CABIN STATE (0 = FOOT, 1 = ENCLOSED, 2 = ROOFLESS/BIKE)
// =========================================================================
13@ = 0 // On-Foot Flag
14@ = 0 // Aircraft Flag
25@ = 0 // Water/Boat Flag
3@ = 0  // Active Player Vehicle Handle

if
    8449: not actor $PLAYER_ACTOR in_a_car
then
    13@ = 1
    26@ = 0 // Context 0: On Foot Outdoors
else
    03C0: 3@ = actor $PLAYER_ACTOR car
    0441: 4@ = car 3@ model
    26@ = 1 // Default: Context 1 (Enclosed Cabin with Roof)

    // Check Motorcycles, Bicycles & Quads
    0E12: get_vehicle 3@ subclass_to 2@
    if or
        0039: 2@ == 7 // Motorcycle
        0039: 2@ == 8 // Bicycle
        0039: 2@ == 2 // Quad Bike
        047A: actor $PLAYER_ACTOR driving_bike
    then
        26@ = 2 // Context 2: Open-Air Bike
    end

    // Check Combat Aircraft (Exclude airliners)
    if or
        04A9: actor $PLAYER_ACTOR driving_heli
        04AB: actor $PLAYER_ACTOR driving_plane
    then
        if and
            8039: not 4@ == 577 // Exclude AT-400
            8039: not 4@ == 592 // Exclude Andromada
        then
            14@ = 1 // Combat Aircraft
        end
    end

    // Check Data-Driven INI Roof Type if not already a bike
    if
        0039: 26@ == 1
    then
        0ADB: 16@v = vehicle_model 4@ name
        0AF0: 4@ = read_int_from_ini_file "cleo\savedata\ShionVehicles.ini" section "VehicleRoofTypes" key 16@v
        if
            0039: 4@ == 1 // Permanent Open-Top
        then
            26@ = 2
        end
        if
            0039: 4@ == 2 // Modular Lowrider Roof
        then
            096D: get_car 3@ component_on_slot 7 model_to 4@
            if
                8019: not 4@ > 0
            then
                26@ = 2
            end
        end
        if
            0039: 4@ == 3 // Dynamic extra1 Frame Node
        then
            if
                8E82: not does_car_have_part_node 3@ "extra1"
            then
                26@ = 2
            end
        end
    end
end

if
    04AD: actor $PLAYER_ACTOR in_water
then
    25@ = 1
end

if
    04A7: actor $PLAYER_ACTOR driving_boat
then
    25@ = 1
end

// Detect vehicles physically floating on water (Vortex Hovercraft & Amphibious water craft)
if and
    0039: 25@ == 0
    0449: actor $PLAYER_ACTOR in_a_car
then
    03C0: 3@ = actor $PLAYER_ACTOR car
    00AA: store_car 3@ position_to 27@ 28@ 29@
    092E: get_water_height_at 27@ 28@ ignore_waves 0 store_to 1@
    if
        0021: 1@ > -100.0
    then
        // 2@ = Vehicle Z - Water Z (Relative elevation above water surface)
        0087: 2@ = 29@
        0063: 2@ -= 1@
        if and
            0021: 2@ > -2.0
            8021: not 2@ > 3.5
        then
            25@ = 1
        end
    end
end

// Check if player or Shion has firearms drawn (Weapons 22 to 38)
23@ = 0
0470: 20@ = actor $PLAYER_ACTOR current_weapon
if and
    20@ >= 22
    20@ <= 38
then
    23@ = 1
end

21@ = 0
if and
    11@ == 0
    10@ > 0
    056D: actor 10@ defined
then
    0470: 21@ = actor 10@ current_weapon
end
if and
    21@ >= 22
    21@ <= 38
then
    23@ = 1
end

// =========================================================================
// PHYSICAL WORLD SENSORS (NO PHANTOMS)
// =========================================================================
00A0: store_actor $PLAYER_ACTOR position_to 27@ 28@ 29@

// Sensor 1: Police Motorcycle (HPV-1000 / Model 523 within 35m, Strictly Ground Roads)
24@ = 0
if and
    0039: 14@ == 0 // Suppressed in aircraft
    0039: 25@ == 0 // Suppressed on water/boats
then
    18@ = 0
    :Wanted_Bike_Scan_Loop
    if
        0AE2: 2@ = random_vehicle_near_point 27@ 28@ 29@ in_radius 35.0 find_next 18@ pass_wrecked 1
    then
        18@ = 1
        if and
            056E: car 2@ defined
            803B: not 2@ == 3@
        then
            046C: 4@ = car 2@ driver
            if
                4@ > 0
            then
                0441: 19@ = car 2@ model
                if
                    19@ == 523
                then
                    24@ = 1
                    goto @Wanted_Bike_Scan_Done
                end
            end
        end
        goto @Wanted_Bike_Scan_Loop
    end
end
:Wanted_Bike_Scan_Done

// Sensor 2: Police / News Helicopter within 100m (Models 497, 488)
12@ = 0
if
    0029: 22@ >= 3
then
    18@ = 0
    :Wanted_Heli_Scan_Loop
    if
        0AE2: 2@ = random_vehicle_near_point 27@ 28@ 29@ in_radius 100.0 find_next 18@ pass_wrecked 1
    then
        18@ = 1
        if and
            056E: car 2@ defined
            803B: not 2@ == 3@
        then
            0441: 19@ = car 2@ model
            if or
                19@ == 497 // Police Maverick
                19@ == 488 // News Chopper
            then
                12@ = 1
                goto @Wanted_Heli_Scan_Done
            end
        end
        goto @Wanted_Heli_Scan_Loop
    end
    :Wanted_Heli_Scan_Done
end

// Sensor 3: Forward Roadblock Sensor (Stationary police vehicles 40m ahead, 30m radius)
9@ = 0
if and
    00DF: actor $PLAYER_ACTOR driving
    0039: 14@ == 0
    0039: 25@ == 0
    0029: 22@ >= 2
then
    0407: store_coords_to 6@ 7@ 1@ from_car 3@ with_offset 0.0 40.0 0.0
    18@ = 0
    :Wanted_Roadblock_Scan_Loop
    if
        0AE2: 2@ = random_vehicle_near_point 6@ 7@ 1@ in_radius 30.0 find_next 18@ pass_wrecked 1
    then
        18@ = 1
        if and
            056E: car 2@ defined
            803B: not 2@ == 3@
        then
            0441: 19@ = car 2@ model
            if or
                19@ == 596 // Police LS
                19@ == 597 // Police SF
                19@ == 598 // Police LV
                19@ == 599 // Police Ranger
                19@ == 427 // Enforcer
                19@ == 433 // Barracks
                19@ == 490 // FBI Rancher
            then
                02E3: 1@ = car 2@ speed
                if
                    8021: not 1@ > 2.5 // Stopped or parked across the road
                then
                    9@ = 1 // Roadblock physically confirmed ahead!
                    goto @Wanted_Roadblock_Scan_Done
                end
            end
        end
        goto @Wanted_Roadblock_Scan_Loop
    end
    :Wanted_Roadblock_Scan_Done
end

// Sensor 4: Rhino Tank within 50m (Model 432 at 6 Stars, Strictly Ground Vehicles or Foot)
17@ = 0
if and
    0029: 22@ >= 6
    0039: 14@ == 0 // Suppressed while flying an aircraft

    18@ = 0
    :Wanted_Tank_Scan_Loop

        0AE2: 2@ = random_vehicle_near_point 27@ 28@ 29@ in_radius 50.0 find_next 18@ pass_wrecked 1

            0441: 19@ = car 2@ model

                goto @Wanted_Tank_Scan_Done
            end

        goto @Wanted_Tank_Scan_Loop
    end
    :Wanted_Tank_Scan_Done
end

// Sensor 5: Military Pursuit Hydra within 300m (Model 520, Aircraft Only at 4+ Stars)
8@ = 0
if and
    0039: 14@ == 1 // Player is piloting a flight vehicle

                goto @Wanted_Hydra_Scan_Done
            end
        end

    end
    :Wanted_Hydra_Scan_Done
end

// =========================================================================
// CENTRAL EXIT ROUTE & LOCK RELEASE
// =========================================================================
:Finalize_Wanted
03EB: clear_small_messages_only
0085: 30@ = 31@
000A: 30@ += MEM_PRIORITY_LOCK
0A8D: 16@ = read_memory 30@ size 4 virtual_protect 0
if
    16@ == 3
then
    0085: 30@ = 31@
    000A: 30@ += MEM_PRIORITY_LOCK
    0A8C: write_memory 30@ size 4 value 0 virtual_protect 0 // Release Priority Lock
end
0A93: terminate_this_custom_script

// =========================================================================
// SUBROUTINE: SAFETY BAILOUT
// =========================================================================
:Check_Alive_Or_Exit
if or
    8256: not player $PLAYER_CHAR defined
    0E25: is_on_cutscene
    0EB7: is_on_scripted_cutscene
then
    0002: jump @Finalize_Wanted
end

if or
    0118: actor $PLAYER_ACTOR dead
    0741: actor $PLAYER_ACTOR busted
then
    0002: jump @Finalize_Wanted
end

// Abort immediately if wanted level dropped to zero (Pay 'n' Spray / Bribe / Lost Heat)
01C0: 22@ = player $PLAYER_CHAR wanted_level
if
    8019: not 22@ > 0
then
    0002: jump @Finalize_Wanted
end

// Check if a Tier 4 (Sister Talk) took over the mic while we slept!
0085: 30@ = 31@
000A: 30@ += MEM_PRIORITY_LOCK
0A8D: 16@ = read_memory 30@ size 4 virtual_protect 0
if
    8039: not 16@ == 3
then
    0A93: terminate_this_custom_script
end

0085: 30@ = 31@
000A: 30@ += MEM_SHION_HANDLE
0A8D: 2@ = read_memory 30@ size 4 virtual_protect 0

if
    2@ == 0 // Inactive or dismissed
then
    0002: jump @Finalize_Wanted
end

if
    2@ > 0 // Only check actor handle if in Solid Ped form!
then
    if or
        856D: not actor 2@ defined
        0118: actor 2@ dead
    then
        0002: jump @Finalize_Wanted
    end

    // If on foot, abort if separated by more than 35 meters
    if
        13@ == 1
    then
        if
            8EE4: not locate_char_distance_to_char $PLAYER_ACTOR char 2@ radius 35.0
        then
            0002: jump @Finalize_Wanted
        end
    else
        // In vehicle: physical Shion must still be in the car!
        if
            00DF: actor $PLAYER_ACTOR driving
        then
            03C0: 3@ = actor $PLAYER_ACTOR car
            if
                80DB: not actor 2@ in_car 3@
            then
                0002: jump @Finalize_Wanted
            end
        end
    end
end
